P4407: 10857: //====================== [P-NEWS-1 E20/E21/E22] news-blackout census =================
P4408: 10858: //--- Print-only. No verdict moves, no selection changes, MTEXIT untouched.
P4409: 10859: //--- E20 pinned table: 11 {eventTimeET, kind} rows transcribed from
P4410: 10860: //--- DRAFT_NEWS-EVENTS-2026.csv @ SHA256 5FFF5C76...EF1F134. No broker-time
P4411: 10861: //--- field, no stored offset: ET wall -> server at read time through
P4412: 10862: //--- TC_ZoneToServer(..., TZ_NEWYORK), the same converter the session
P4413: 10863: //--- windows use (US + server DST rules inside TickCore, evaluated on the
P4414: 10864: //--- naive wall timestamp; event hours 08:30/14:00 ET never touch the
P4415: 10865: //--- 02:00 transition hours, so the documented boundary-hour bound cannot
P4416: 10866: //--- engage these rows). Accessors are switch-based: no init order, no
P4417: 10867: //--- global-constructor dependence.
P4418: 10868: //--- E21: SrjInNewsBlackout is THE predicate body. The census calls it now;
P4419: 10869: //--- the exit side and the entry side will call the same body later. There
P4420: 10870: //--- is no second implementation (E21 halt-1 satisfied by construction).
P4421: 10871: //--- Window [newsBarOpen - 1*PS, newsBarOpen + 2*PS); newsBar containment
P4422: 10872: //--- [barOpen, barOpen + PS). No literal minutes: the shape is PS multiples;
P4423: 10873: //--- the /60 in offsetMinutes is a seconds-to-minutes unit, not a window term.
P4424: 10874: #define SRJ_NEWS_ROWS 11
P4425: 10875: #define SHADOW_NEWS true
P4426: 10876: //--- Pilot range (server frame) for inWindow membership. From RECON1_P1 per
P4427: 10877: //--- the packet's "full window" term — NOT derived from Bars()/iTime: those
P4428: 10878: //--- return the full broker history depth (years), and iTime(0) at lazy-init
P4429: 10879: //--- is the first bar, so data-derived bounds exclude every later window
P4430: 10880: //--- (RECON12 measured rowsInWindow=0 on exactly this defect). If the packet
P4431: 10881: //--- ever moves window, these two lines move with it.
P4432: 10882: #define SRJ_PILOT_FROM D'2026.08.26 00:00'
P4433: 10883: #define SRJ_PILOT_TO   D'2026.09.10 00:00'
P4434: 10884: string SrjNewsET(const int i)
P4435: 10885:    {
P4436: 10886:     switch(i)
P4437: 10887:       {
P4438: 10888:        case  0: return "2026-09-04 08:30";
P4439: 10889:        case  1: return "2026-09-11 08:30";
P4440: 10890:        case  2: return "2026-09-16 14:00";
P4441: 10891:        case  3: return "2026-10-02 08:30";
P4442: 10892:        case  4: return "2026-10-14 08:30";
P4443: 10893:        case  5: return "2026-10-28 14:00";
P4444: 10894:        case  6: return "2026-11-06 08:30";
P4445: 10895:        case  7: return "2026-11-10 08:30";
P4446: 10896:        case  8: return "2026-12-04 08:30";
P4447: 10897:        case  9: return "2026-12-09 14:00";
P4448: 10898:        case 10: return "2026-12-10 08:30";
P4449: 10899:       }
P4450: 10900:     return "";
P4451: 10901:    }
P4452: 10902: string SrjNewsKind(const int i)
P4453: 10903:    {
P4454: 10904:     switch(i)
P4455: 10905:       {
P4456: 10906:        case  0: return "NFP";
P4457: 10907:        case  1: return "CPI";
P4458: 10908:        case  2: return "FOMC";
P4459: 10909:        case  3: return "NFP";
P4460: 10910:        case  4: return "CPI";
P4461: 10911:        case  5: return "FOMC";
P4462: 10912:        case  6: return "NFP";
P4463: 10913:        case  7: return "CPI";
P4464: 10914:        case  8: return "NFP";
P4465: 10915:        case  9: return "FOMC";
P4466: 10916:        case 10: return "CPI";
P4467: 10917:       }
P4468: 10918:     return "";
P4469: 10919:    }
P4470: 10920: //--- "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM" -> naive wall datetime. StringToTime is server-frame
P4471: 10921: //--- so the pinned format is parsed manually; the result is NEVER passed to
P4472: 10922: //--- iTime/CopyBuffer without TC_ZoneToServer first.
P4473: 10923: datetime SrjNewsEtWall(const int i)
P4474: 10924:    {
P4475: 10925:     string s = SrjNewsET(i);
P4476: 10926:     int y  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 0, 4);
P4477: 10927:     int mo = (int)StringSubstr(s, 5, 2);
P4478: 10928:     int d  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 8, 2);
P4479: 10929:     int h  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 11, 2);
P4480: 10930:     int mi = (int)StringSubstr(s, 14, 2);
P4481: 10931:     return TC_MakeTime(y, mo, d, h, mi);
P4482: 10932:    }
P4483: 10933: datetime g_news_newsBar[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4484: 10934: datetime g_news_winS[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4485: 10935: datetime g_news_winE[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4486: 10936: int      g_news_offMin[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4487: 10937: int      g_news_inWin[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4488: 10938: int      g_news_halted[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4489: 10939: int      g_news_rowSnap[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4490: 10940: int      g_news_nWin = 0;
P4491: 10941: int      g_news_overlaps = 0;
P4492: 10942: string   g_news_overlapList = "";
P4493: 10943: bool     g_news_init = false;
P4494: 10944: int      g_news_memberBars = 0;
P4495: 10945: int      g_news_slimbInWin = 0;
P4496: 10946: int      g_news_s5InWin = 0;
P4497: 10947: int      g_news_flatNews = 0;
P4498: 10948: int      g_news_flatDay = 0;
P4499: 10949: int      g_news_flatWeek = 0;
P4500: 10950: datetime g_news_dayMarks[32];
P4501: 10951: int      g_news_dayDone[32];
P4502: 10952: int      g_news_dayN = 0;
P4503: 10953: string   g_news_friET = "";
P4504: 10954: datetime g_news_friMarks[8];
P4505: 10955: int      g_news_friDone[8];
P4506: 10956: int      g_news_friN = 0;
P4507: 10957: bool SrjInNewsBlackout(const datetime barOpen, string &kindOut, int &posOut, int &rowOut)
P4508: 10958:    {
P4509: 10959:     kindOut = "-"; posOut = -1; rowOut = -1;
P4510: 10960:     if(!g_news_init) return false;
P4511: 10961:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4512: 10962:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4513: 10963:       {
P4514: 10964:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 0 || g_news_halted[i] == 1) continue;
P4515: 10965:        if(barOpen < g_news_winS[i] || barOpen >= g_news_winE[i]) continue;
P4516: 10966:        kindOut = SrjNewsKind(i); rowOut = i;
P4517: 10967:        if(barOpen < g_news_newsBar[i]) posOut = 0;
P4518: 10968:        else if(barOpen < g_news_newsBar[i] + ps) posOut = 1;
P4519: 10969:        else posOut = 2;
P4520: 10970:        return true;
P4521: 10971:       }
P4522: 10972:     return false;
P4523: 10973:    }
P4524: 10974: //--- Lazy init on the first closed bar. Conversions need no history; the E21
P4525: 10975: //--- gap check does NOT run here (tester series only extend to the current
P4526: 10976: //--- bar — September bars don't exist yet on the first August bar, and a
P4527: 10977: //--- check now would false-halt; RECON12b measured exactly that). Pilot
P4528: 10978: //--- range is SRJ_PILOT_FROM/TO.
P4529: 10979: void SrjNewsInit()
P4530: 10980:    {
P4531: 10981:     if(g_news_init) return;
P4532: 10982:     g_news_init = true;
P4533: 10983:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4534: 10984:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4535: 10985:       {
P4536: 10986:        datetime wall = SrjNewsEtWall(i);
P4537: 10987:        datetime srv  = TC_ZoneToServer(wall, TZ_NEWYORK);
P4538: 10988:        g_news_offMin[i] = (int)(((long)srv - (long)wall) / 60);
P4539: 10989:        datetime nb = (datetime)(((long)srv / ps) * ps);
P4540: 10990:        g_news_newsBar[i] = nb;
P4541: 10991:        g_news_winS[i] = (datetime)((long)nb - ps);
P4542: 10992:        g_news_winE[i] = (datetime)((long)nb + 2 * ps);
P4543: 10993:        g_news_inWin[i] = (g_news_winE[i] > SRJ_PILOT_FROM && g_news_winS[i] < SRJ_PILOT_TO) ? 1 : 0;
P4544: 10994:        g_news_halted[i] = 0;
P4545: 10995:        g_news_rowSnap[i] = 0;
P4546: 10996:       }
P4547: 10997:     //--- Day marks: 16:55 ET (= 17:00 daily close minus 5 min, the dayFlat
P4548: 10998:     //--- census definition, quoted in BLACKOUT_CENSUS) per calendar date in
P4549: 10999:     //--- range; Friday marks: 17:00 ET (the weekFlat census definition). Both
P4550: 11000:     //--- resolved through the same converter. Noon-dow is zone-safe: at
P4551: 11001:     //--- midday the ET and server dates always agree.
P4552: 11002:     g_news_dayN = 0;
P4553: 11003:     g_news_friN = 0;
P4554: 11004:     g_news_friET = "";
P4555: 11005:     datetime cur = TC_DayStart(SRJ_PILOT_FROM);
P4556: 11006:     while(cur < SRJ_PILOT_TO && g_news_dayN < 32)
P4557: 11007:       {
P4558: 11008:        MqlDateTime dd; TimeToStruct(cur, dd);
P4559: 11009:        g_news_dayMarks[g_news_dayN] = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 16, 55), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4560: 11010:        g_news_dayDone[g_news_dayN] = 0;
P4561: 11011:        g_news_dayN++;
P4562: 11012:        MqlDateTime noon; TimeToStruct(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 12, 0), noon);
P4563: 11013:        if(noon.day_of_week == 5 && g_news_friN < 8)
P4564: 11014:          {
P4565: 11015:           g_news_friMarks[g_news_friN] = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 17, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4566: 11016:           g_news_friDone[g_news_friN] = 0;
P4567: 11017:           g_news_friN++;
P4568: 11018:           string one = StringFormat("%04d-%02d-%02d 17:00", dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day);
P4569: 11019:           g_news_friET += ((g_news_friET == "") ? "" : "|") + one;
P4570: 11020:          }
P4571: 11021:        cur = TC_ShiftDayStart(cur, 1);
P4572: 11022:       }
P4573: 11023:    }
P4574: 11024: //--- "Open" for the flat census. g_mtrade.active is STICKY: it is set at
P4575: 11025: //--- fill and cleared only by the next fill's MtReset, so a closed trade
P4576: 11026: //--- still reads active. state goes MT_CLOSED at close (normal path). Both
P4577: 11027: //--- conditions together are the true open interval (RECON12b overcounted
P4578: 11028: //--- dayFlat 12 and weekFlat 2 on the sticky flag alone).
P4579: 11029: bool SrjNewsIsOpen()
P4580: 11030:    {
P4581: 11031:     return (g_mtrade.active && g_mtrade.state != MT_CLOSED);
P4582: 11032:    }
P4583: 11033: //--- End-of-run row finalization. Full history exists now, so the E21 gap
P4584: 11034: //--- check is sound: the event instant must sit inside a real bar (exact
P4585: 11035: //--- containment, never snapped). Halted rows print HALT plus their ROW
P4586: 11036: //--- with inWindow=0 and are excluded from every expectation.
P4587: 11037: void SrjNewsFinalize()
P4588: 11038:    {
P4589: 11039:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4590: 11040:     g_news_nWin = 0;
P4591: 11041:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4592: 11042:       {
P4593: 11043:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 1)
P4594: 11044:          {
P4595: 11045:           datetime srv = TC_ZoneToServer(SrjNewsEtWall(i), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4596: 11046:           if(iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, srv, true) < 0)
P4597: 11047:             {
P4598: 11048:              g_news_halted[i] = 1; g_news_inWin[i] = 0;
P4599: 11049:              string haltLine = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_HALT_ROW kind=%s eventTimeET=%s reason=GAP_NO_BAR",
P4600: 11050:                                             SrjNewsKind(i), SrjNewsET(i));
P4601: 11051:              LwAudit("BLACKOUT_HALT", haltLine);
P4602: 11052:              Print(haltLine);
P4603: 11053:             }
P4604: 11054:           else
P4605: 11055:              g_news_nWin++;
P4606: 11056:          }
P4607: 11057:        int spanned = (int)(((long)g_news_winE[i] - (long)g_news_winS[i]) / ps);
P4608: 11058:        string rl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_ROW kind=%s eventTimeET=%s newsBarOpen=%s windowStart=%s windowEnd=%s offsetMinutes=%d inWindow=%d barsSpanned=%d",
P4609: 11059:                                 SrjNewsKind(i), SrjNewsET(i),
P4610: 11060:                                 TimeToString(g_news_newsBar[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4611: 11061:                                 TimeToString(g_news_winS[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4612: 11062:                                 TimeToString(g_news_winE[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4613: 11063:                                 g_news_offMin[i], g_news_inWin[i], spanned);
P4614: 11064:        LwAudit("BLACKOUT_ROW", rl);
P4615: 11065:        Print(rl);
P4616: 11066:       }
P4617: 11067:     for(int a = 0; a < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; a++)
P4618: 11068:         for(int b = a + 1; b < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; b++)
P4619: 11069:            if(g_news_inWin[a] == 1 && g_news_inWin[b] == 1 &&
P4620: 11070:               g_news_winS[a] < g_news_winE[b] && g_news_winS[b] < g_news_winE[a])
P4621: 11071:              { g_news_overlaps++; g_news_overlapList += ((g_news_overlaps > 1) ? "|" : "") + SrjNewsKind(a) + "@" + SrjNewsET(a) + "x" + SrjNewsKind(b) + "@" + SrjNewsET(b); }
P4622: 11072:    }
P4623: 11073: //--- Per-closed-bar census hook. Read-only: counts and prints only. Called at
P4624: 11074: //--- the END of OnTick so g_mtrade reflects the settled post-eval state.
P4625: 11075: void SrjNewsOnBar(const datetime barTime)
P4626: 11076:    {
P4627: 11077:     string k; int pos, row;
P4628: 11078:     if(SrjInNewsBlackout(barTime, k, pos, row))
P4629: 11079:       {
P4630: 11080:        g_news_memberBars++;
P4631: 11081:        string bl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_BAR barTime=%s kind=%s pos=%s",
P4632: 11082:                                 TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), k,
P4633: 11083:                                 (pos == 0) ? "PRE" : ((pos == 1) ? "NEWS" : "POST"));
P4634: 11084:        LwAudit("BLACKOUT_BAR", bl);
P4635: 11085:        Print(bl);
P4636: 11086:       }
P4637: 11087:     //--- Flat populations, edge-triggered, once each. "Open" = SrjNewsIsOpen().
P4638: 11088:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4639: 11089:       {
P4640: 11090:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 0 || g_news_rowSnap[i] == 1) continue;
P4641: 11091:        if(barTime >= g_news_winS[i])
P4642: 11092:          { g_news_rowSnap[i] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatNews++; }
P4643: 11093:       }
P4644: 11094:     for(int d = 0; d < g_news_dayN; d++)
P4645: 11095:       {
P4646: 11096:        if(g_news_dayDone[d] == 1) continue;
P4647: 11097:        if(barTime >= g_news_dayMarks[d])
P4648: 11098:          { g_news_dayDone[d] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatDay++; }
P4649: 11099:       }
P4650: 11100:     for(int f = 0; f < g_news_friN; f++)
P4651: 11101:       {
P4652: 11102:        if(g_news_friDone[f] == 1) continue;
P4653: 11103:        if(barTime >= g_news_friMarks[f])
P4654: 11104:          { g_news_friDone[f] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatWeek++; }
P4655: 11105:       }
P4656: 11106:    }
P4657: 11107: 
P4658: 11108: //====================== OnInit =========================================
P4659: 11109: int OnInit()
P4660: 11110:   {
P4661: 11111:    InitAuthorityTable();
P4662: 11112: 
P4663: 11113:    if(!(bool)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER)) TC_DetectServerOffset();
P4664: 11114:    TC_DetectServerOffsetNow();
P4665: 11115: 
P4666: 11116:    {
P4667: 11117:     datetime srvNow   = TimeCurrent();
P4668: 11118:     datetime gmtNow   = TimeGMT();
P4669: 11119:     bool     inTester = (bool)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER);
P4670: 11120: 
P4671: 11121:     MqlDateTime d;
P4672: 11122:     TimeToStruct(TC_DayStart(srvNow), d);
P4673: 11123: 
P4674: 11124:     datetime lonFromSrv = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  2, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4675: 11125:     datetime lonToSrv   = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  5, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4676: 11126:     datetime nyFromSrv  = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  7, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4677: 11127:     datetime nyToSrv    = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day, 12, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4678: 11128: 
P4679: 11129:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] TimeCurrent=%s TimeGMT=%s srvMinusGmt=%+ds "
P4680: 11130:                 "gtc_serverGmtBase=%+ds offsetNow=%s tester=%s",
P4681: 11131:                 TimeToString(srvNow, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS),
P4682: 11132:                 TimeToString(gmtNow, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS),
P4683: 11133:                 (int)((long)srvNow - (long)gmtNow),
P4684: 11134:                 gtc_serverGmtBase,
P4685: 11135:                 TC_ServerOffsetText(),
P4686: 11136:                 (inTester ? "YES" : "NO"));
P4687: 11137: 
P4688: 11138:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] session windows resolved to SERVER frame from ET date "
P4689: 11139:                 "%04d.%02d.%02d:  LONDON 02:00-05:00 ET = %s .. %s   |   "
P4690: 11140:                 "NYAM 07:00-12:00 ET = %s .. %s",
P4691: 11141:                 d.year, d.mon, d.day,
P4692: 11142:                 TimeToString(lonFromSrv, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4693: 11143:                 TimeToString(lonToSrv,   TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4694: 11144:                 TimeToString(nyFromSrv,  TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4695: 11145:                 TimeToString(nyToSrv,    TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
P4696: 11146: 
P4697: 11147:     if(srvNow <= 0 || gmtNow <= 0)
P4698: 11148:        Print("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] WARNING: TimeCurrent() or TimeGMT() returned <= 0...");
P4699: 11149: 
P4700: 11150:     if(inTester && srvNow == gmtNow)
P4701: 11151:        Print("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] WARNING: TESTER RUN with TimeGMT()==TimeCurrent()...");
P4702: 11152:    }
P4703: 11153: 
P4704: 11154:    ResetLastError();
P4705: 11155:    g_hPoi = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpPoiMarkerName,
P4706: 11156:                     InpPoi_UseSeed, InpPoi_BinPips, InpPoi_WeightMode);
P4707: 11157:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] POI handle=%d err=%d", g_hPoi, GetLastError());
P4708: 11158:    ResetLastError();
P4709: 11159:    g_hCqd = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpCqdName,
P4710: 11160:                     PERIOD_D1, InpCqd_NoReset, true,
P4711: 11161:                     InpCqd_MaxCarryBars, InpCqd_MaxBackfillDays);
P4712: 11162:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] CQD handle=%d err=%d", g_hCqd, GetLastError());
P4713: 11163:    ResetLastError();
P4714: 11164:    g_hFlow = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpFlowLogicName,
P4715: 11165:                      1, InpFL_HtfLookbackBars,
P4716: 11166:                      //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE1] confirmed selection (F252
P4717: 11167:                      //--- inUseConfirmedHTFOnly; EU preservation sibling row
P4718: 11168:                      //--- grades the global effect).
P4719: 11169:                      PERIOD_H4, PERIOD_H1, PERIOD_M15, true, 60);
P4720: 11170:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] Flow handle=%d err=%d", g_hFlow, GetLastError());
P4721: 11171:     if(g_hPoi == INVALID_HANDLE || g_hCqd == INVALID_HANDLE || g_hFlow == INVALID_HANDLE)
P4722: 11172:       { Print("[SRJ-EA] OnInit FAILED: one or more iCustom handles are invalid."); return INIT_FAILED; }
P4723: 11173:     //--- [P-SEL-1 E51] platform-Fractals handles (own pair; NEVER in the
P4724: 11174:     //--- run-blocking conjunction above - a SEL handle failure reports
P4725: 11175:     //--- SELHALT end-of-run, it never stops the baseline run).
P4726: 11176:     ResetLastError();
P4727: 11177:     g_selfracM5 = iFractals(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4728: 11178:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] SEL fractal handle M5=%d err=%d", g_selfracM5, GetLastError());
P4729: 11179:     ResetLastError();
P4730: 11180:     g_selfracH1 = iFractals(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1);
P4731: 11181:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] SEL fractal handle H1=%d err=%d", g_selfracH1, GetLastError());
P4732: 11182:     SrjSideNote("INIT", g_dir);
P4733: 11183:     ResetSequence();
P4734: 11184:     //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E13 + amendment, P-SLDEF-4 E34] FRAME_NOTE, once per run:
P4735: 11185:     //--- the six conventions a later session could silently invert. (1) Slot
P4736: 11186:     //--- frame: every printed shift is ReadFlow frame, CopyBuffer position =
P4737: 11187:    //--- every printed shift is ReadFlow frame, CopyBuffer position =
P4738: 11188:    //--- eval shift + FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET (the settled slot). (2) Apex frame: a
P4739: 11189:    //--- swing value at eval shift s is priced at ApexShift(s) = s + offset;
P4740: 11190:    //--- barTime(s) prints iTime at that price shift - the code's own belief.
P4741: 11191:    //--- (3) Protective sign: raw deltaPts is (ref - today) in points, so
P4742: 11192:    //--- LONG-protective prints negative and SHORT-protective positive;
P4743: 11193:    //--- outwardPts = LONG ? -delta : +delta normalizes to outward-positive.
P4744: 11194:    //--- (4) Population identity [P-NEWS-1 verdict rule]: every gate count
P4745: 11195:    //--- names its population in the same sentence - input-side (guard
P4746: 11196:    //--- applications, raw encounters) vs result-side (post-walk values).
P4747: 11197:     //--- Labels (bar=, barTime) are server time via iTime/TimeToString.
P4748: 11198:     //--- (6) Read windows per reader [P-SLDEF-4 E31]: the ladder reads from
P4749: 11199:     //--- the entry bar to the deepest rung-obligated slot plus margin
P4750: 11200:     //--- (SLADWIN ladWindowStart/ladWindowSpan/ladReadLimit); the OB walk
P4751: 11201:     //--- reads walkStart..walkStart+500, the fractal walk guardStart..
P4752: 11202:     //--- guardStart+500 (SLADWIN walkWinOB/walkWinFR). A ladder window and a
P4753: 11203:     //--- walk window are DIFFERENT windows: coverage is by slot reach, and
P4754: 11204:     //--- ladLimitHit rows are UNCOVERED_READ_LIMIT, never silent.
P4755: 11205:     //--- (7) Origin per site [P-ADOPT-1 E48]: S5 = strict next-open
P4756: 11206:     //--- (caller-stamped entry bar; a stale stamp halts the row, never
P4757: 11207:     //--- substitutes); S2POLL/S3ARM = eval-bar close (the shared
P4758: 11208:     //--- memo-path convention both sites' live R already uses).
P4759: 11209:     //--- THRESHOLD: the live minimum-R is an artifact of the run. Source is
P4760: 11210:    //--- "ini" when the value differs from the compiled default (an ini-set
P4761: 11211:    //--- 1.0 is indistinguishable - recorded as compiled_default).
P4762: 11212:      string frame_note = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] FRAME_NOTE offset=%d apex=s+%d labels=server-time "
P4763: 11213:                  "protectiveSign=LONG-lower/SHORT-higher outward=LONG(-d)/SHORT(+d) "
P4764: 11214:                  "populations=inputVsResultNamed ladObligN=6refs ladCovers=oblig+ext1 "
P4765: 11215:                  "sigmap=signalTime-s5BarTime-PeriodSeconds "
P4766: 11216:                  "readwin=ladder:entryBar+span|walkOB:walkStart+500|walkFR:guardStart+500 "
P4767: 11217:                  "origin=S5:nextOpen-strictHalt|S2POLL+S3ARM:evalClose "
P4768: 11218:                  "THRESHOLD minRewardRisk=%.2f source=%s",
P4769: 11219:                FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET, FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET, InpMinRewardRisk,
P4770: 11220:                ((InpMinRewardRisk == 1.0) ? "compiled_default" : "ini"));
P4771: 11221:    LwAudit("FRAME_NOTE", frame_note);
P4772: 11222:    Print(frame_note);
P4773: 11223:    //--- [P-NEWS-1 E20] pinned-table note, once per run. Digest is a source
P4774: 11224:    //--- string constant (the human check); the BLACKOUT_ROW emissions at the
P4775: 11225:    //--- first bar are the audit artifact. No history needed here.
P4776: 11226:    string table_note = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] TABLE_NOTE rows=%d digest=%s anchor=%s",
P4777: 11227:                                     SRJ_NEWS_ROWS,
P4778: 11228:                                     "5FFF5C762DABCAB7811C598D8B16942BBCBD5F9D94C91D0BB54CF8C36EF1F134",
P4779: 11229:                                     "21:00_broker");
P4780: 11230:     LwAudit("TABLE_NOTE", table_note);
P4781: 11231:     Print(table_note);
P4782: 11232:     //--- [P-ORIGIN-1 §2/FREEZE] site-origin manifest, once per run.
P4783: 11233:     //--- Candidate bindings resolve ONLY at the seven frozen example
P4784: 11234:     //--- bars (SrjOriginEntry); UNBOUND elsewhere — no fallback, and
P4785: 11235:     //--- diagnostic rows exist only at example bars by construction.
P4786: 11236:     //--- Per-example bindings + rule declaration live in
P4787: 11237:     //--- BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md (frozen pre-execution).
P4788: 11238:     string sl60_m1 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=S5-direct role=compute existingBinding=stamped-strict-next-open candidateRule=frozen-HAND-entry-at-R1-R5-else-UNBOUND memoRel=none freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4789: 11239:     string sl60_m2 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=S2POLL role=compute+memo-write existingBinding=eval-bar-close candidateRule=frozen-HAND-entry-at-T1-T2-else-UNBOUND memoRel=writes-gen-stamped freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4790: 11240:     string sl60_m3 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=S3ARM role=compute+memo-write+read existingBinding=eval-bar-close candidateRule=UNBOUND-no-example-bars memoRel=writes-on-COMPUTE-reads-on-HIT freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4791: 11241:     string sl60_m4 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=memo-read role=read existingBinding=HIT-returns-stored candidateRule=not-applicable memoRel=HIT-reports-req-vs-stored-plus-genID freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4792: 11242:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m1); Print(sl60_m1);
P4793: 11243:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m2); Print(sl60_m2);
P4794: 11244:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m3); Print(sl60_m3);
P4795: 11245:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m4); Print(sl60_m4);
P4796: 11246:     Print("[SRJ-EA] Initialised.");
P4797: 11247:    return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
P4798: 11248:   }

P4826: 11276:                   "s2 fail=%d empty=%d neg=%d zero=%d pos=%d other=%d",

P5282: 11732: //--- [P-SLDEF-3 E29] MTFLIP: one line per HTF_FLIP verdict evaluation

P5323: **EX-4. Exit priority block and managed-trade price setup - EA 11826-11842 and EA 11851-11912.**
P5324:
P5325: ```mql5
P5326: 11826:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] MTCLOSE bar=%s leg=%s ticket=%I64u magic=%I64d action=%d retcode=%d deal=%I64u closepid=%I64d closeentry=%d entryPid=%I64d flat=%d ref=%s",
P5327: 11827:                TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), leg, ticket, pmagic,

P5334: 11834: // Spec section 4 site 3: the exit, evaluated at the NEXT candle's open. Called once
P5335: 11835: // per closed bar from OnTick AFTER the entry pipeline (section 7's separation: this
P5336: 11836: // function never touches the entry pipeline or any working-set field). Every verdict
P5337: 11837: // is logged (instrumentation-first, section 4's mitigation); an actual exit also
P5338: 11838: // emits the EXIT alert (ALERT-ONLY preserved - never an order). Same-bar priority
P5339: 11839: // when several tests fire together: SL, then TP_TOUCH, then POI_BODY_BREAK, then
P5340: 11840: // HTF_FLIP (when re-enabled; beats DAY_CLOSE on shared bars), then DAY_CLOSE
P5341: 11841: // (universal scope: every managed trade) (the conservative stop-first standard; MTEXIT/MTLIFE record terminal
P5342: 11842: // exit reasons so the operator can re-judge any instance).
P5343: ```
P5344: ```mql5
P5345: 11851:    double o = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5346: 11852:    double h = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5347: 11853:    double l = iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5348: 11854:    double c = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5349: 11855:    //--- the NEXT candle's open = the evaluation instant's price (section 4);
P5350: 11856:    //--- fail-soft to the evaluated bar's close if the next open cannot be read.
P5351: 11857:    double nextOpenPx = (barShift >= 1) ? iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift - 1) : 0.0;
P5352: 11858:    if(nextOpenPx <= 0.0) nextOpenPx = c;
P5353: 11859:    double bodyLo = MathMin(o, nextOpenPx);
P5354: 11860:    double bodyHi = MathMax(o, nextOpenPx);
P5355: 11861:    double EPS = 0.001 * _Point;   // the T161K float guard, threshold-free semantics
P5356: 11862: 
P5357: 11863:    //--- PENDING_FILL (section 5.5): fill on the first touch of the entry level.
P5358: 11864:    //--- Under the next-open entry the fill bar's own open IS the entry, so this
P5359: 11865:    //--- fills at the first evaluation; the branch keeps the lifecycle complete.
P5360: 11866:    if(g_mtrade.state == MT_PENDING_FILL)
P5361: 11867:      {
P5362: 11868:       bool touched = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG) ? (l <= g_mtrade.entryPrice + EPS)
P5363: 11869:                                                 : (h >= g_mtrade.entryPrice - EPS);
P5364: 11870:       if(!touched)
P5365: 11871:         {
P5366: 11872:          //--- not filled yet: cancel on a bias flip (the three-flag conjunction is
P5367: 11873:          //--- the same event per sections 3.4/5.5)
P5368: 11874:          double ltfBias;
P5369: 11875:          if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_LTF_BIAS, ltfBias, barShift))
P5370: 11876:            {
P5371: 11877:             int want = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG) ? 1 : -1;
P5372: 11878:             if((int)MathRound(ltfBias) != want)
P5373: 11879:               {
P5374: 11880:                g_mtrade.state      = MT_CLOSED;
P5375: 11881:                g_mtrade.exitReason = MT_EXIT_CANCEL_BIAS;
P5376: 11882:                g_mtrade.exitBarTime = barTime;
P5377: 11883:                g_mtrade.exitPrice   = nextOpenPx;
P5378: 11884:                 if(InpDebugLog)

P5409: **EX-5. Retarget, model touch, day-close verdict and verdict print - EA 11939-12025.**
P5410:
P5411: ```mql5
P5412: 11939: 
P5413: 11940:    //--- (c) the body-close exit: PRICE's BODY close through a BEHIND trigger line
P5414: 11941:    //--- (body = open -> next open, the T161K convention; "it must be body" - Q5).
P5415: 11942:    //--- A line's own gap/move alone never exits (Q3; section 1.3). Side is per bar
P5416: 11943:    //--- (section 5.2): a trigger line whose CURRENT value sits ahead of the trade
P5417: 11944:    //--- is a touch-target, not a body-close trigger. The census logs ALL twelve
P5418: 11945:    //--- lines per bar so every MT_EXIT_SCOPE variant is measurable from one run.
P5419: 11946:    for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P5420: 11947:      {
P5421: 11948:        double L;
P5422: 11949:        if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, k, L, barShift)) continue;
P5423: 11950:        if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0) continue;
P5424: 11951:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E14] same N1 body counter at the exit site. Grounding:
P5425: 11952:        //--- break needs bodyLo < L-EPS (LONG) / bodyHi > L+EPS (SHORT), both
P5426: 11953:        //--- strict: exact equality never breaks.
P5427: 11954:        if(bodyLo == L || bodyHi == L) g_n1_poiEqBody++;
P5428: 11955:        bool behind = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG)  ? (L < nextOpenPx)
P5429: 11956:                                                  : (L > nextOpenPx);
P5430: 11957:       bool through = false;
P5431: 11958:       if(behind)
P5432: 11959:         {
P5433: 11960:          if(g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG)  through = (bodyLo < L - EPS);
P5434: 11961:          else                          through = (bodyHi > L + EPS);
P5435: 11962:         }
P5436: 11963:        bool isTrigger = MtIsBreakTrigger(k);
P5437: 11964:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1b E19] exit-site pairing: the line verdict is known here.
P5438: 11965:        //--- Strictness says equality never sets `through`, so every paired
P5439: 11966:        //--- instance is expected ok (survived); a BREAK coincidence reports inv.
P5440: 11967:        if(bodyLo == L || bodyHi == L)
P5441: 11968:          { if(isTrigger && behind && through) g_n1_exitBodyInv++; else g_n1_exitBodySurv++; }
P5442: 11969:        if(InpDebugLog)
P5443: 11970:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] EXITCENSUS bar=%s dir=%s line=%s val=%s side=%s "
P5444: 11971:                      "trigger=%d bodyLo=%s bodyHi=%s verdict=%s",
P5445: 11972:                      TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P5446: 11973:                      DirName(g_mtrade.dir),
P5447: 11974:                      g_lineCode[k], DoubleToString(L, _Digits),
P5448: 11975:                      (behind ? "behind" : "ahead"),
P5449: 11976:                      (int)isTrigger,
P5450: 11977:                      DoubleToString(bodyLo, _Digits),
P5451: 11978:                      DoubleToString(bodyHi, _Digits),
P5452: 11979:                      (isTrigger && behind && through) ? "BREAK" : "ok");
P5453: 11980:       //--- [P-EXITRANK-6] anchor-rank gate (his 2026-09-23 rule: same-line cross never exits; only HIGHER-authority breaks exit; lower number = higher authority; amends charter 9.1(2) same-line case, supersedes E3).
P5454: 11981:       if(isTrigger && behind && through && !vBREAK && g_mtrade.anchorLine >= 0 && g_mtrade.anchorLine < POI_NLINES && g_authorityRank[k] < g_authorityRank[g_mtrade.anchorLine])
P5455: 11982:         {
P5456: 11983:          vBREAK = true;
P5457: 11984:          breakLineVal  = L;
P5458: 11985:          breakLineName = g_lineCode[k];
P5459: 11986:         }
P5460: 11987:      }
P5461: 11988: 
P5462: 11989:    //--- (e) section 5.6: the HTF aggregate flip exits TREND-following trades
P5463: 11990:    double mtlH = 0.0, mtlM = 0.0, mtlL = 0.0; // [P-HTFLOG] the HTF leg values (diagnostic)
P5464: 11991:    int    mtlWant = 0, mtlAnti = -1;          // [P-HTFLOG] anti=-1 => the leg block did not run

P5586:
P5587: ```mql5
P5588: 1893: bool UjClosedSessionTarget(const SManagedTrade &t, const int barShift, double &px)
P5589: 1894:    {
P5590: 1895:     px = 0.0;
P5591: 1896:     if(t.dir != DIR_LONG && t.dir != DIR_SHORT) return false;
P5592: 1897:     int es = t.sessionAtEntry;
P5593: 1898:     if(es != SESSION_LONDON && es != SESSION_NYAM) return false;
P5594: 1899:     if(t.uj_admitBarTime <= 0) return false;
P5595: 1900:      datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5596: 1901:      if(bt == 0) return false;
P5597: 1902:      if((int)CurrentTradingWindow(bt) == es) return false;
P5598: 1903:      datetime bp = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P5599: 1904:      if(bp == 0) return false;
P5600: 1905:      if((int)CurrentTradingWindow(bp) != es) return false;  // first-out-bar pin (V347 Luna-2): barShift must be the first bar outside the entry-session run
P5601: 1906:      double ext = 0.0;
P5602: 1907:     bool have = false;
P5603: 1908:     bool uj_ended = false;
P5604: 1909:     datetime uj_newest = 0;
P5605: 1910:     datetime uj_oldest = 0;
P5606: 1911:      for(int k = barShift + 1; k < barShift + 601; k++)
P5607: 1912:        {
P5608: 1913:         datetime bk = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, k);
P5609: 1914:         if(bk == 0) break;
P5610: 1915:         if((int)CurrentTradingWindow(bk) != es)
P5611: 1916:           {
P5612: 1917:            if(have) uj_ended = true;
P5613: 1918:            break;  // contiguous-run end (V347 Luna-2): the walk starts inside an es run by the barShift+1 pin; admission containment is the separate P097 test, so any non-es bar ends the walk - fail-closed when no price seen yet
P5614: 1919:           }
P5615: 1920:        if(uj_newest == 0) { uj_newest = bk; uj_oldest = bk; }
P5616: 1921:        else uj_oldest = bk;
P5617: 1922:        double v = (t.dir == DIR_LONG) ? iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, k) : iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, k);
P5618: 1923:        if(v <= 0.0) continue;
P5619: 1924:        if(!have || (t.dir == DIR_LONG && v > ext) || (t.dir == DIR_SHORT && v < ext)) { ext = v; have = true; }
P5620: 1925:       }
P5621: 1926:     if(!have || !uj_ended || ext <= 0.0) return false;
P5622: 1927:     if(t.uj_admitBarTime < uj_oldest || t.uj_admitBarTime > uj_newest) return false;
P5623: 1928:     px = ext;
P5624: 1929:     return true;
P5625: 1930:    }
P5626: ```

P5791: ```mql5
P5792: 12070: 
P5793: 12071: //====================== [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8] UJ closure implementation ============
P5794: 12072: //--- print helper: EMPTY-safe double
P5795: 12073: string UjDbl(double v)
P5796: 12074:   {
P5797: 12075:    if(v == EMPTY_VALUE) return "EMPTY";
P5798: 12076:    return DoubleToString(v, 1);
P5799: 12077:   }
P5800: 12078: //--- day key YYYY.MM.DD at broker midnight (F327-328 alignment, EA-side)
P5801: 12079: string UjDayKey(datetime t)
P5802: 12080:   {
P5803: 12081:    if(t <= 0) return "";
P5804: 12082:    return TimeToString(StringToTime(TimeToString(t, TIME_DATE)), TIME_DATE);
P5805: 12083:   }
P5806: 12084: //--- whole-day difference bar-day minus key-day (-1 when unkeyed)
P5807: 12085: int UjDayDiff(datetime barT, string dayKey)
P5808: 12086:   {
P5809: 12087:    if(barT <= 0 || dayKey == "") return -1;
P5810: 12088:    return (int)((StringToTime(TimeToString(barT, TIME_DATE)) - StringToTime(dayKey)) / 86400);
P5811: 12089:   }
P5812: 12090: //--- shared 1R assertion (single print schema; callers abort on false)
P5813: 12091: bool SrjUjAssert1R(double entry, double sl, double tp, string barKey, string src,
P5814: 12092:                    double &riskOut, double &rewardOut, double &ROut)
P5815: 12093:   {
P5816: 12094:    int d = 0;
P5817: 12095:    if(tp > entry && sl < entry) d = 1;
P5818: 12096:    else if(tp < entry && sl > entry) d = -1;
P5819: 12097:    double risk = -1.0, reward = -1.0, R = -1.0;
P5820: 12098:    bool ok = false;
P5821: 12099:    if(d != 0)
P5822: 12100:      {
P5823: 12101:       risk = (d == 1) ? (entry - sl) : (sl - entry);
P5824: 12102:       reward = (d == 1) ? (tp - entry) : (entry - tp);
P5825: 12103:       R = (risk > 0.0) ? (reward / risk) : -1.0;
P5826: 12104:       ok = (risk > 0.0 && reward > 0.0 && reward >= risk);
P5827: 12105:      }
P5828: 12106:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJ1R bar=%s src=%s entry=%s sl=%s tp=%s risk=%s reward=%s R=%.2f verdict=%s",
P5829: 12107:                barKey, src, DoubleToString(entry, _Digits), DoubleToString(sl, _Digits),
P5830: 12108:                DoubleToString(tp, _Digits), DoubleToString(risk, _Digits),
P5831: 12109:                DoubleToString(reward, _Digits), R, (ok ? "PASS" : "FAIL"));
P5832: 12110:    riskOut = risk; rewardOut = reward; ROut = R;
P5833: 12111:    return ok;
P5834: 12112:   }
P5835: 12113: //--- pool consumability: READY for the election day (SHORT/EMPTY/FAILED/stale: no-consume)
P5836: 12114: bool SrjUjPoolConsumable(string dayKey)
P5837: 12115:   {
P5838: 12116:    return (uj_pubState == UJ_POOL_READY && dayKey != "" && uj_poolDayKey == dayKey);
P5839: 12117:   }
P5840: 12118: //--- history walker: builds temp pool over [2026.04.29, today]
P5841: 12119: int SrjHistPoolBuild(SUjPoolRec &out[], string &achStart, int &dayCnt, int &famRead, int &unavail, int &emptyValid)
P5842: 12120:   {
P5843: 12121:    ArrayResize(out, 0);
P5844: 12122:    achStart = ""; dayCnt = 0; famRead = 0; unavail = 0; emptyValid = 0;
P5845: 12123:    datetime reqD = StringToTime("2026.04.29");
P5846: 12124:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5847: 12125:    if(nowB <= 0 || reqD <= 0) return UJ_POOL_FAILED;
P5848: 12126:    datetime todayD = StringToTime(TimeToString(nowB, TIME_DATE));
P5849: 12127:    datetime maxOrigin = todayD - 2 * 86400;
P5850: 12128:    string sessName[4] = { "ASIA", "LONDON", "NY", "PM" };
P5851: 12129:    bool truncated = false;
P5852: 12130:    for(datetime D = reqD; D <= todayD; D += 86400)
P5853: 12131:      {
P5854: 12132:       datetime refT = D + 43200;
P5855: 12133:       int sh = iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, refT, false);
P5856: 12134:       if(sh < 0) { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5857: 12135:       datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, sh);
P5858: 12136:       if(bt <= 0) { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5859: 12137:       if(StringToTime(TimeToString(bt, TIME_DATE)) != D) continue;
P5860: 12138:       dayCnt++;
P5861: 12139:       if(achStart == "") achStart = TimeToString(D, TIME_DATE);
P5862: 12140:       bool dayEmpty = true;
P5863: 12141:       for(int s = 0; s < 4; s++)
P5864: 12142:         {
P5865: 12143:          double vLH = 0.0, vLL = 0.0, vPH = 0.0, vPL = 0.0;
P5866: 12144:          if(!ReadFlow(10 + s * 2, vLH, sh) || !ReadFlow(11 + s * 2, vLL, sh) ||
P5867: 12145:             !ReadFlow(40 + s * 2, vPH, sh) || !ReadFlow(41 + s * 2, vPL, sh))
P5868: 12146:            { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5869: 12147:          famRead += 4;
P5870: 12148:          double lv[4]; datetime ov[4]; string sv[4];
P5871: 12149:          lv[0] = vLH; ov[0] = D; sv[0] = "LIVE";
P5872: 12150:          lv[1] = vLL; ov[1] = D; sv[1] = "LIVE";
P5873: 12151:          lv[2] = vPH; ov[2] = D - 86400; sv[2] = "PD";
P5874: 12152:          lv[3] = vPL; ov[3] = D - 86400; sv[3] = "PD";
P5875: 12153:          for(int r = 0; r < 4; r++)
P5876: 12154:            {
P5877: 12155:             if(lv[r] == EMPTY_VALUE || lv[r] <= 0.0) continue;
P5878: 12156:             if(ov[r] < reqD || ov[r] > maxOrigin) continue;
P5879: 12157:             int side = ((r == 0 || r == 2) ? 0 : 1);
P5880: 12158:             string okey = TimeToString(ov[r], TIME_DATE);
P5881: 12159:             bool dup = false;
P5882: 12160:             for(int e = 0; e < ArraySize(out); e++)
P5883: 12161:               { if(out[e].dayKey == okey && out[e].sess == sessName[s] && out[e].side == side) { dup = true; break; } }
P5884: 12162:             if(dup) continue;
P5885: 12163:             int n = ArraySize(out);
P5886: 12164:             ArrayResize(out, n + 1);
P5887: 12165:             out[n].dayKey = okey; out[n].sess = sessName[s]; out[n].side = side;
P5888: 12166:             out[n].value = lv[r]; out[n].source = sv[r];
P5889: 12167:             out[n].closure = ov[r] + 86399; out[n].poolGen = uj_poolGen + 1;
P5890: 12168:             dayEmpty = false;
P5891: 12169:            }
P5892: 12170:          if(truncated) break;
P5893: 12171:         }
P5894: 12172:       if(truncated) break;
P5895: 12173:       if(dayEmpty) emptyValid++;
P5896: 12174:      }
P5897: 12175:    if(truncated) return (ArraySize(out) > 0 ? UJ_POOL_SHORT : UJ_POOL_FAILED);
P5898: 12176:    return UJ_POOL_READY;
P5899: 12177:   }
P5900: 12178: //--- unconditional refresh in the new-bar path (day-keyed rollover rebuild)
P5901: 12179: void SrjUjPoolRefresh()
P5902: 12180:   {
P5903: 12181:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5904: 12182:    string todayK = UjDayKey(nowB);
P5905: 12183:    if(todayK != "" && todayK == uj_poolDayKey && uj_pubState == UJ_POOL_READY)
P5906: 12184:      {
P5907: 12185:       if(InpDebugLog)
P5908: 12186:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLCOV req=%s ach=%s days=%d famRead=%d unavail=%d emptyValid=%d state=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d cadence=no-rebuild",
P5909: 12187:                      uj_reqStart, uj_achStart, uj_dayCount, uj_famRead, uj_unavail,
P5910: 12188:                      uj_emptyValid, uj_pubState, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen);
P5911: 12189:       return;
P5912: 12190:      }
P5913: 12191:    uj_attemptState = UJ_POOL_BUILDING; uj_attemptCount++;
P5914: 12192:    SUjPoolRec tmp[];
P5915: 12193:    string ach = ""; int dc = 0, fr = 0, un = 0, ev = 0;
P5916: 12194:    int st = SrjHistPoolBuild(tmp, ach, dc, fr, un, ev);
P5917: 12195:    uj_attemptState = st;
P5918: 12196:    uj_reqStart = "2026.04.29"; uj_achStart = ach;
P5919: 12197:    uj_dayCount = dc; uj_famRead = fr; uj_unavail = un; uj_emptyValid = ev;
P5920: 12198:    if(st == UJ_POOL_READY)
P5921: 12199:      {
P5922: 12200:       int n = ArraySize(tmp);
P5923: 12201:       ArrayResize(uj_pool, n);
P5924: 12202:       for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
P5925: 12203:         {
P5926: 12204:          uj_pool[i].dayKey = tmp[i].dayKey; uj_pool[i].sess = tmp[i].sess;
P5927: 12205:          uj_pool[i].side = tmp[i].side; uj_pool[i].value = tmp[i].value;
P5928: 12206:          uj_pool[i].source = tmp[i].source; uj_pool[i].closure = tmp[i].closure;
P5929: 12207:          uj_pool[i].poolGen = tmp[i].poolGen;
P5930: 12208:         }
P5931: 12209:       uj_poolDayKey = todayK; uj_pubState = UJ_POOL_READY; uj_poolGen++;
P5932: 12210:      }
P5933: 12211:    //--- SHORT/FAILED: published pool untouched (last-good; stale never consumed:
P5934: 12212:    //--- the consumability gate requires day match + READY).
P5935: 12213:    if(InpDebugLog)
P5936: 12214:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLCOV req=%s ach=%s days=%d famRead=%d unavail=%d emptyValid=%d state=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d cadence=rebuild",
P5937: 12215:                   uj_reqStart, ach, dc, fr, un, ev, st, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen);
P5938: 12216:    if(InpDebugLog)
P5939: 12217:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLSTATE dayKey=%s publishedPoolState=%d refreshAttemptState=%d poolGen=%d",
P5940: 12218:                   uj_poolDayKey, uj_pubState, uj_attemptState, uj_poolGen);
P5941: 12219:   }
P5942: 12220: //--- run-level pool-service record (zero admissions print the schema, never vacuous)
P5943: 12221: void SrjUjPoolFinalize()
P5944: 12222:   {
P5945: 12223:    if(!InpDebugLog) return;
P5946: 12224:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5947: 12225:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLSVC requestedStart=%s achievedStart=%s runEnd=%s dayCount=%d familyRead=%d unavailable=%d emptyValid=%d finalState=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d admissions=%d",
P5948: 12226:                uj_reqStart, uj_achStart, UjDayKey(nowB), uj_dayCount, uj_famRead,
P5949: 12227:                uj_unavail, uj_emptyValid, uj_attemptState, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen, uj_admitCount);
P5950: 12228:   }
P5951: 12229: //--- single probe printer (pass bar-time join key; M15 row gated on M15-new-bar)
P5952: 12230: void SrjUjProbeTuple(int barShift, datetime barTime)
P5953: 12231:   {
P5954: 12232:    string bk = TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES);
P5955: 12233:    double h4 = EMPTY_VALUE, h1 = EMPTY_VALUE, m15 = EMPTY_VALUE, ltf = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5956: 12234:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_HIGH, h4, barShift)) h4 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5957: 12235:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_MID, h1, barShift)) h1 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5958: 12236:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_LOW, m15, barShift)) m15 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5959: 12237:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_LTF_BIAS, ltf, barShift)) ltf = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5960: 12238:    //--- probe-side DIV classifier: verbatim firing-walk bound, full domain
P5961: 12239:    int maxWalk = Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT) - 1;
P5962: 12240:    int readFail = 0, emptyV = 0, zeroV = 0, latestNZ = 0;
P5963: 12241:    string kind = "-";
P5964: 12242:    bool complete = true;
P5965: 12243:    for(int s = barShift; s <= maxWalk; s++)
P5966: 12244:      {
P5967: 12245:       double verdict = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5968: 12246:       if(!ReadBuf1(g_hCqd, CQD_BUF_DIVVERDICT, verdict, s)) { readFail++; complete = false; continue; }
P5969: 12247:       if(verdict == EMPTY_VALUE) { emptyV++; continue; }
P5970: 12248:       int v = (int)MathRound(verdict);
P5971: 12249:       if(v == 0) { zeroV++; continue; }
P5972: 12250:       if(latestNZ == 0) { latestNZ = v; kind = ((MathAbs(v) == 1) ? "regular" : "hidden"); }
P5973: 12251:      }
P5974: 12252:    bool aligned = ((g_dir == DIR_LONG && (latestNZ == 1 || latestNZ == 2)) ||
P5975: 12253:                    (g_dir == DIR_SHORT && (latestNZ == -1 || latestNZ == -2)));
P5976: 12254:    string cls = (!complete ? "INCOMPLETE" : (latestNZ == 0 ? "ABSENT" : (aligned ? "ALIGNED" : "OPPOSING")));
P5977: 12255:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPROBE bar_key=%s h4=%s h1=%s m15=%s confirmedFeed=1 ltf=%s div=%s kind=%s readFail=%d empty=%d zero=%d complete=%d latestNZ=%d covReq=%s covAch=%s dayCount=%d ticktime=%s lag=chartTime-1bar",
P5978: 12256:                bk, UjDbl(h4), UjDbl(h1), UjDbl(m15), UjDbl(ltf), cls, kind,
P5979: 12257:                readFail, emptyV, zeroV, (complete ? 1 : 0), latestNZ,
P5980: 12258:                ((uj_reqStart == "") ? "-" : uj_reqStart), ((uj_achStart == "") ? "-" : uj_achStart),
P5981: 12259:                uj_dayCount, TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS));
P5982: 12260:    //--- IE10A: M15 buffer-row print fires only on M15-new-bar ticks
P5983: 12261:    static datetime uj_lastM15 = 0;
P5984: 12262:    datetime m15t = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 0);
P5985: 12263:    if(m15t <= 0)
P5986: 12264:      { if(InpDebugLog) PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJM15RF bar_key=%s", bk); }
P5987: 12265:    else if(uj_lastM15 == 0)
P5988: 12266:      { uj_lastM15 = m15t;
P5989: 12267:        PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJM15ROW bar_key=%s m15time=%s m15vote=%s", bk, TimeToString(m15t, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), UjDbl(m15)); }
P5990: 12268:    else if(m15t != uj_lastM15)
P5991: 12269:      { uj_lastM15 = m15t;
P5992: 12270:        PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJM15ROW bar_key=%s m15time=%s m15vote=%s", bk, TimeToString(m15t, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), UjDbl(m15)); }
P5993: 12271:   }
P5994: 12272: 
P5995: 12273: //====================== OnTick =========================================
P5996: 12274: void OnTick()
P5997: 12275:   {
P5998: 12276:    static datetime s_lastBarTime = 0;
P5999: 12277:    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P6000: 12278:    if(currentBarTime == s_lastBarTime) return;
P6001: 12279:    s_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
P6002: 12280:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5] unconditional pool refresh in the new-bar path
P6003: 12281:    SrjUjPoolRefresh();
P6004: 12282:    LoadWorkingSet(1, currentBarTime);
P6005: 12283:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE9/IE4] boundary order: memo-clear, probe-print,
P6006: 12284:    //--- then evaluation (non-load-bearing beyond this sentence).
P6007: 12285:    uj_memo_valid = false; uj_memo_barTime = 0; uj_memo_src = "";
P6008: 12286:    SrjUjProbeTuple(1, currentBarTime);
P6009: 12287:    EvaluateClosedBar(1, currentBarTime);
P6010: 12288:    StoreWorkingSet(1, currentBarTime);
P6011: 12289:    //--- [P-EXITMODEL] the section 4 site-3 exit phase: runs AFTER the entry pipeline
P6012: 12290:    //--- and AFTER the working-set store (it touches NO working-set field - section 7
P6013: 12291:    //--- separation). Evaluates the managed trade at the NEXT candle's open.
P6014: 12292:    EvaluateManagedTrade(1);
P6015: 12293:    //--- [P-NEWS-1 E22] blackout census hook, last: observes the settled state.
P6016: 12294:    SrjNewsInit();
P6017: 12295:    if(InpDebugLog && SHADOW_NEWS)
P6018: 12296:       SrjNewsOnBar(currentBarTime);
P6019: 12297:   }
P6020: 12298: //+------------------------------------------------------------------+
P6021: ```

P6023: **EX-14. Sizing, stops gate and order send - EA 10762-10795 and EA 10803-10807.**
P6024:
P6025: ```mql5
P6026: 10762:       double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
P6027: 10763:       double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
P6028: 10764: 
P6029: 10765:       if(slDistanceReal > 0 && tickSize > 0)
P6030: 10766:         {

P6274:
P6275: ```mql5
P6276: 10975: //--- gap check does NOT run here (tester series only extend to the current
P6277: 10976: //--- bar — September bars don't exist yet on the first August bar, and a
P6278: 10977: //--- check now would false-halt; RECON12b measured exactly that). Pilot
P6279: 10978: //--- range is SRJ_PILOT_FROM/TO.
P6280: 10979: void SrjNewsInit()
P6281: 10980:    {
P6282: 10981:     if(g_news_init) return;
P6283: 10982:     g_news_init = true;
P6284: 10983:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P6285: 10984:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P6286: 10985:       {
P6287: 10986:        datetime wall = SrjNewsEtWall(i);
P6288: 10987:        datetime srv  = TC_ZoneToServer(wall, TZ_NEWYORK);
P6289: 10988:        g_news_offMin[i] = (int)(((long)srv - (long)wall) / 60);
P6290: 10989:        datetime nb = (datetime)(((long)srv / ps) * ps);
P6291: 10990:        g_news_newsBar[i] = nb;
P6292: 10991:        g_news_winS[i] = (datetime)((long)nb - ps);
P6293: 10992:        g_news_winE[i] = (datetime)((long)nb + 2 * ps);
P6294: 10993:        g_news_inWin[i] = (g_news_winE[i] > SRJ_PILOT_FROM && g_news_winS[i] < SRJ_PILOT_TO) ? 1 : 0;
P6295: 10994:        g_news_halted[i] = 0;
P6296: 10995:        g_news_rowSnap[i] = 0;
P6297: ```
P6298: ```mql5
P6299: 11025: //--- fill and cleared only by the next fill's MtReset, so a closed trade
P6300: 11026: //--- still reads active. state goes MT_CLOSED at close (normal path). Both
P6301: 11027: //--- conditions together are the true open interval (RECON12b overcounted
P6302: 11028: //--- dayFlat 12 and weekFlat 2 on the sticky flag alone).
P6303: 11029: bool SrjNewsIsOpen()
P6304: 11030:    {
P6305: 11031:     return (g_mtrade.active && g_mtrade.state != MT_CLOSED);
P6306: 11032:    }
P6307: 11033: //--- End-of-run row finalization. Full history exists now, so the E21 gap
P6308: 11034: //--- check is sound: the event instant must sit inside a real bar (exact
P6309: 11035: //--- containment, never snapped). Halted rows print HALT plus their ROW
P6310: 11036: //--- with inWindow=0 and are excluded from every expectation.
P6311: ```

P6313: 10808:          if(!tradeResult)

P6342: 10837:               }

P6357: **EX-26. Touch-discovery body - EA 6830-6920.** New at V403. Sonnet's A6 and GLM's condition 1 both refuse a promotion claim until this body is on the page, because it is the only thing that can set the touch state on a bullish confirmation bar.
P6358:
P6359: ```mql5
P6360: 6830: bool FindLegTouch(int barShift, double zHi, double zLo,
P6361: 6831:                   int &foundShiftOut, double &legTimeOut, bool fromFvg)
P6362: 6832:   {
P6363: 6833:    foundShiftOut = -1;
P6364: 6834:    legTimeOut    = 0.0;
P6365: 6835:    if(!(zHi > 0.0 && zLo > 0.0)) return false;
P6366: 6836: 
P6367: 6837:    double legT;
P6368: 6838:    if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_STRUCT_LEG_TIME, legT, barShift) &&
P6369: 6839:       legT > 0.0 && legT != EMPTY_VALUE)
P6370: 6840:       legTimeOut = legT;
P6371: 6841:    datetime legBoundary = (legTimeOut > 0.0) ? (datetime)legTimeOut : 0;
P6372: 6842: 
P6373: 6843:    for(int s = barShift; s <= barShift + 500; s++)
P6374: 6844:      {
P6375: 6845:       datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6376: 6846:       if(bt <= 0)                              break;
P6377: 6847:       if(legBoundary > 0 && bt < legBoundary)   break;
P6378: 6848: 
P6379: 6849:       double so = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6380: 6850:       double sh = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6381: 6851:       double sl = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6382: 6852:       double sc = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6383: 6853: 
P6384: 6854:       bool oppositeDir = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? (sc < so) : (sc > so);
P6385: 6855:       bool touchesZone = (sh >= zLo && sl <= zHi);
P6386: 6856:       if(oppositeDir && (!fromFvg || touchesZone))
P6387: 6857:         { foundShiftOut = s; return true; }
P6388: 6858:      }
P6389: 6859:    return false;
P6390: 6860:   }
P6391: 6861: 
P6392: 6862: //====================== [Task 55 / EA-56 + EA-58] Zone in-play test ===
P6393: 6863: //--- Ruling 8's in-play test, extracted so it can be applied to MORE THAN ONE
P6394: 6864: //--- candidate zone on the same bar. Logic is character-equivalent to the test
P6395: 6865: //--- the S3 block already runs inline: BAR penetration of the zone by the
P6396: 6866: //--- evaluation bar's own range, else SWING1 containment (freshest confirmed
P6397: 6867: //--- swing on the protective side), else SWING2 containment (next DISTINCT
P6398: 6868: //--- confirmed swing on that same side).
P6399: 6869: //---
P6400: 6870: //--- No distance parameter, no bar-count parameter, no tolerance. The _Point
P6401: 6871: //--- separation test and the 500-slot bound match FindNearestSwing, the 2-swing
P6402: 6872: //--- stop branch and the S3 inline test exactly; they are safety limits, not
P6403: 6873: //--- tunable thresholds, so Part A section 7 is not engaged.
P6404: 6874: //---
P6405: 6875: //--- Silent by design. The caller reports the verdicts (see ZONEPICK), so this
P6406: 6876: //--- adds no per-bar journal output of its own.
P6407: 6877: //---
P6408: 6878: //--- The S3 block's inline copy and ZoneAdoptable's copy are NOT removed. That
P6409: 6879: //--- consolidation is EA-49 and belongs to the state-machine rewrite.
P6410: 6880: bool ZoneInPlay(int barShift, double zHi, double zLo,
P6411: 6881:                 double stopRef, bool haveStop)
P6412: 6882:   {
P6413: 6883:    if(!(zHi > 0.0 && zLo > 0.0)) return false;
P6414: 6884: 
P6415: 6885:    double bHi = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6416: 6886:    double bLo = iLow (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6417: 6887:    if(bHi >= zLo && bLo <= zHi) return true;
P6418: 6888: 
P6419: 6889:    int    buf = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? FL_BUF_SWING_LOW : FL_BUF_SWING_HIGH;
P6420: 6890:    double sw1 = 0.0;
P6421: 6891:    int    sh1 = -1;
P6422: 6892:    if(!FindNearestSwing(buf, barShift, sw1, sh1)) return false;
P6423: 6893:    if(sw1 >= zLo && sw1 <= zHi) return true;
P6424: 6894: 
P6425: 6895:    //--- [STEP 1 / charter ruling 3] In-play depth is the SL LEG: every confirmed
P6426: 6896:    //--- protective-side swing from the evaluation bar back to the stop reference
P6427: 6897:    //--- chosen by ComputeSlReference. The stop swing itself is tested and then
P6428: 6898:    //--- terminates the walk - the one verified operator zone was put in play by
P6429: 6899:    //--- the second swing, the one the stop was placed at. Without a stop
P6430: 6900:    //--- reference this bar, the measured two-swing depth (SWING2) remains the
P6431: 6901:    //--- bound, per council Part 1.1. No distance parameter; the bounds are the
P6432: 6902:    //--- stop reference (structural), history exhaustion (bt<=0), and the _Point
P6433: 6903:     //--- distinctness test - safety limits and structure, never thresholds.
P6434: 6904:     //--- [P-TRIM-S2POLL E3] loop-invariant hoist. Bars() cannot change within one
P6435: 6905:     //--- EvaluateClosedBar pass. Matches the existing t127_limit2 / t133_limit idiom.
P6436: 6906:     const int zip_limit = barShift + Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P6437: 6907:     if(!haveStop)
P6438: 6908:      {
P6439: 6909:       for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= sh1 + 500; s++)
P6440: 6910:         {
P6441: 6911:          double v2;
P6442: 6912:          if(!ReadFlow(buf, v2, s))          break;
P6443: 6913:          if(v2 == EMPTY_VALUE || v2 <= 0.0) continue;
P6444: 6914:          if(MathAbs(v2 - sw1) <= _Point)    continue;
P6445: 6915:          return (v2 >= zLo && v2 <= zHi);
P6446: 6916:         }
P6447: 6917:       return false;
P6448: 6918:      }
P6449: 6919:     double prev = sw1;
P6450: 6920:     for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= zip_limit; s++)
P6451: ```

P6453: **EX-27. Qualifying-zone reader and zone-adoption bodies - EA 6654-6829.** New at V403. These carry the zero-bound behaviour that the zeroed zone produces, which GLM's condition 8 requires be classified rather than relied upon.
P6454:
P6455: ```mql5
P6456: 6654: bool ReadQualifyingZone(int barShift, double &zHiOut, double &zLoOut, bool &fromFvgOut,
P6457: 6655:                         double stopRef, bool haveStop)
P6458: 6656:   {
P6459: 6657:    double xobHi = 0.0, xobLo = 0.0, fvgHi = 0.0, fvgLo = 0.0;
P6460: 6658: 
P6461: 6659:    // [Task 105] Identity census at the S4 re-read site. Placed immediately
P6462: 6660:    // after the zone locals are declared and before any read or return, so it
P6463: 6661:    // cannot alter the function's result on any path. Prints ids only; the
P6464: 6662:    // bounds for this bar are already printed by ZONEMOVE and ZONEADOPT, so
P6465: 6663:    // cross-referencing by bar= is sufficient and no duplicated read is needed.
P6466: 6664:    // -1 means the buffer read failed. 0 means FlowLogic selected no object.
P6467: 6665:    double t105a_xobId = -1.0, t105a_fvgId = -1.0;
P6468: 6666:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_XOB_OBJ_ID, t105a_xobId, barShift)) t105a_xobId = -1.0;
P6469: 6667:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_FVG_OBJ_ID, t105a_fvgId, barShift)) t105a_fvgId = -1.0;
P6470: 6668:    if(InpDebugLog)
P6471: 6669:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] ZONEID bar=%s site=S4RQZ xobId=%d fvgId=%d",
P6472: 6670:                   TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6473: 6671:                   (int)t105a_xobId, (int)t105a_fvgId);
P6474: 6672:                   
P6475: 6673:    bool haveXob = ReadFlow(FL_BUF_XOB_ZONE_HIGH, xobHi, barShift) && xobHi != EMPTY_VALUE &&
P6476: 6674:                   ReadFlow(FL_BUF_XOB_ZONE_LOW,  xobLo, barShift) && xobLo != EMPTY_VALUE;
P6477: 6675:    bool haveFvg = ReadFlow(FL_BUF_FVG_LEG_ZONE_HIGH, fvgHi, barShift) && fvgHi != EMPTY_VALUE &&
P6478: 6676:                   ReadFlow(FL_BUF_FVG_LEG_ZONE_LOW,  fvgLo, barShift) && fvgLo != EMPTY_VALUE;
P6479: 6677: 
P6480: 6678:    //--- [STEP 1 / council Part 2.2] This site previously returned the FVG
P6481: 6679:    //--- unconditionally, with no in-play test applied here. With the widened walk
P6482: 6680:    //--- live at S3, a not-in-play FVG could displace an in-play XOB on this
P6483: 6681:    //--- re-read, making the widened admission reversible one bar later. Fix:
P6484: 6682:    //--- when BOTH zones are exported, the FVG yields to an in-play XOB exactly
P6485: 6683:    //--- when the FVG itself is not in play. Strictly monotone: every bar that
P6486: 6684:    //--- returned the XOB before still does; a lone FVG still returns as before.
P6487: 6685:    if(haveFvg && haveXob)
P6488: 6686:      {
P6489: 6687:       bool fvgInPlay = ZoneInPlay(barShift, MathMax(fvgHi, fvgLo), MathMin(fvgHi, fvgLo), stopRef, haveStop);
P6490: 6688:       bool xobInPlay = ZoneInPlay(barShift, MathMax(xobHi, xobLo), MathMin(xobHi, xobLo), stopRef, haveStop);
P6491: 6689:       if(InpDebugLog)
P6492: 6690:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] RQZPICK bar=%s site=S4RQZ fvgInPlay=%d xobInPlay=%d downgraded=%d",
P6493: 6691:                      TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6494: 6692:                      (int)fvgInPlay, (int)xobInPlay,
P6495: 6693:                      (int)((!fvgInPlay && xobInPlay) ? 1 : 0));
P6496: 6694:       if(!fvgInPlay && xobInPlay)
P6497: 6695:         { zHiOut = MathMax(xobHi, xobLo); zLoOut = MathMin(xobHi, xobLo); fromFvgOut = false; return true; }
P6498: 6696:      }
P6499: 6697:    if(haveFvg)
P6500: 6698:      { zHiOut = MathMax(fvgHi, fvgLo); zLoOut = MathMin(fvgHi, fvgLo); fromFvgOut = true;  return true; }
P6501: 6699:    if(haveXob)
P6502: 6700:      { zHiOut = MathMax(xobHi, xobLo); zLoOut = MathMin(xobHi, xobLo); fromFvgOut = false; return true; }
P6503: 6701:    return false;
P6504: 6702:   }
P6505: 6703: 
P6506: 6704: //====================== [Task 36 / EA-47] Zone adoption guard =========
P6507: 6705: //--- Measured cause: on 2026.08.20 three of six ZONEMOVEs adopted
P6508: 6706: //--- 1.16547-1.16595 with obSwing=1.16536 - byte-identical to 08.19's values.
P6509: 6707: //--- SRJ_NearestPromotedOBIndex selects the largest startBar among promoted,
P6510: 6708: //--- valid, activated in-bias order blocks; when nothing from the current day
P6511: 6709: //--- qualifies it returns the previous day's. One of those adoptions cleared a
P6512: 6710: //--- legitimate touch (TOUCHCLEAR 18:55). Part A Step 4 requires "the current,
P6513: 6711: //--- freshest structure", so a zone ~200 points from price with no structure
P6514: 6712: //--- touching it is not adoptable mid-sequence.
P6515: 6713: //---
P6516: 6714: //--- The test is Ruling 8's in-play test, unchanged and threshold-free: BAR,
P6517: 6715: //--- SWING1, or SWING2 penetration of the candidate zone. Verified against the
P6518: 6716: //--- Task 35 journal - the 09:20 adoption of the operator's own XOB passes on
P6519: 6717: //--- BAR (close 1.16775 inside 1.16737-1.16778) and all three stale adoptions
P6520: 6718: //--- fail on all three conditions.
P6521: 6719: //---
P6522: 6720: //--- This does NOT re-adjudicate the existing zone. The `same` short-circuit
P6523: 6721: //--- returns true when the zone has not moved, so Task 35's boundary - in-play
P6524: 6722: //--- gates arming and is not re-checked afterwards - is preserved. What is
P6525: 6723: //--- gated here is a REPLACEMENT, which would otherwise bypass Ruling 7b's
P6526: 6724: //--- arming gate entirely.
P6527: 6725: //---
P6528: 6726: //--- The logic duplicates the S3 block's inline in-play test rather than
P6529: 6727: //--- sharing it, because extracting that block is a restructure and belongs to
P6530: 6728: //--- the state-machine rewrite. Logged as EA-49.
P6531: 6729: bool ZoneAdoptable(int barShift, double zHi, double zLo,
P6532: 6730:                    double stopRef, bool haveStop)
P6533: 6731:   {
P6534: 6732:    if(!(zHi > 0.0 && zLo > 0.0)) return false;
P6535: 6733: 
P6536: 6734:    if(MathAbs(zHi - g_zoneHi) <= _Point * 0.5 &&
P6537: 6735:       MathAbs(zLo - g_zoneLo) <= _Point * 0.5)
P6538: 6736:       return true;
P6539: 6737: 
P6540: 6738:    bool   ok  = false;
P6541: 6739:    string via = "none";
P6542: 6740:    double bHi = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6543: 6741:    double bLo = iLow (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6544: 6742: 
P6545: 6743:    if(bHi >= zLo && bLo <= zHi) { ok = true; via = "BAR"; }
P6546: 6744: 
P6547: 6745:    int    buf = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? FL_BUF_SWING_LOW : FL_BUF_SWING_HIGH;
P6548: 6746:    double sw1 = 0.0, sw2 = 0.0;
P6549: 6747:    int    sh1 = -1,  sh2 = -1;
P6550: 6748: 
P6551: 6749:    if(FindNearestSwing(buf, barShift, sw1, sh1))
P6552: 6750:      {
P6553: 6751:        if(sw1 >= zLo && sw1 <= zHi)
P6554: 6752:          { ok = true; if(via == "none") via = "SWING1"; }
P6555: 6753: 
P6556: 6754:        //--- [P-TRIM-S2POLL E3] loop-invariant hoist. Bars() cannot change within one
P6557: 6755:        //--- EvaluateClosedBar pass. Matches the existing t127_limit2 / t133_limit idiom.
P6558: 6756:        const int za_limit = barShift + Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P6559: 6757:        if(!haveStop)
P6560: 6758:         {
P6561: 6759:          for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= sh1 + 500; s++)
P6562: 6760:            {
P6563: 6761:             double v2;
P6564: 6762:             if(!ReadFlow(buf, v2, s))          break;
P6565: 6763:             if(v2 == EMPTY_VALUE || v2 <= 0.0) continue;
P6566: 6764:             if(MathAbs(v2 - sw1) <= _Point)    continue;
P6567: 6765:             sw2 = v2; sh2 = s;
P6568: 6766:             break;
P6569: 6767:            }
P6570: 6768: 
P6571: 6769:          if(sw2 > 0.0 && sw2 >= zLo && sw2 <= zHi)
P6572: 6770:            { ok = true; if(via == "none") via = "SWING2"; }
P6573: 6771:         }
P6574: 6772:       else
P6575: 6773:         {
P6576: 6774:          //--- [STEP 1] same SL-leg depth as ZoneInPlay: every confirmed
P6577: 6775:          //--- protective-side swing back to the stop reference, which ends the leg.
P6578: 6776:           double prev = sw1;
P6579: 6777:           for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= za_limit; s++)
P6580: 6778:            {
P6581: 6779:             double v2;
P6582: 6780:             if(!ReadFlow(buf, v2, s))          break;
P6583: 6781:             if(v2 == EMPTY_VALUE || v2 <= 0.0) continue;
P6584: 6782:             if(MathAbs(v2 - prev) <= _Point)   continue;
P6585: 6783:             prev = v2;
P6586: 6784:             if(v2 >= zLo && v2 <= zHi)
P6587: 6785:               { ok = true; if(via == "none") via = "SWINGLEG"; }
P6588: 6786:             if((g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? (v2 <= stopRef) : (v2 >= stopRef)) break;
P6589: 6787:            }
P6590: 6788:         }
P6591: 6789:      }
P6592: 6790: 
P6593: 6791:    if(InpDebugLog)
P6594: 6792:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] ZONEADOPT bar=%s dir=%s adopt=%d via=%s newLo=%s newHi=%s "
P6595: 6793:                   "barLo=%s barHi=%s sw1=%s@%d sw2=%s@%d",
P6596: 6794:                   TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6597: 6795:                   DirName(g_dir), (int)ok, via,
P6598: 6796:                   DoubleToString(zLo, _Digits),
P6599: 6797:                   DoubleToString(zHi, _Digits),
P6600: 6798:                   DoubleToString(bLo, _Digits),
P6601: 6799:                   DoubleToString(bHi, _Digits),
P6602: 6800:                   (sw1 > 0.0 ? DoubleToString(sw1, _Digits) : "-"), sh1,
P6603: 6801:                   (sw2 > 0.0 ? DoubleToString(sw2, _Digits) : "-"), sh2);
P6604: 6802:    return ok;
P6605: 6803:   }
P6606: 6804: 
P6607: 6805: //====================== [Task 52 / EA-59b] Leg-scoped touch scan ======
P6608: 6806: //--- Operator ruling EA-59b (Option B, wide): the opposite-direction touch may be
P6609: 6807: //--- ANY older bar within the current structural leg, not only a bar evaluated
P6610: 6808: //--- after the candidate armed. Admission is judged on the confirming bar with
P6611: 6809: //--- every condition simultaneously true; the touch is a leg-scoped fact, not a
P6612: 6810: //--- recency test, and retracement depth is not an admission criterion.
P6613: 6811: //---
P6614: 6812: //--- Measured cause: on 2026.08.20 the 09:35 candidate armed while evaluating the
P6615: 6813: //--- operator's own 09:30 entry bar - arming timing was correct - but the
P6616: 6814: //--- retracement into the zone had already completed before arming. The old S4
P6617: 6815: //--- test only ever examined the single bar it was evaluating, so no touch was
P6618: 6816: //--- ever seen and the sequence died at state=S4_ARMED. The EA was structurally
P6619: 6817: //--- one full touch-and-confirm cycle behind the operator, every time.
P6620: 6818: //---
P6621: 6819: //--- Bound is the STRUCTURAL leg boundary (buffer 30), read through ReadFlow so it
P6622: 6820: //--- lands in the settled slot. When the export reads 0.0 - unset, which is
P6623: 6821: //--- warmup only since UpstreamReady gates evaluation - the 500-slot cap is the
P6624: 6822: //--- sole bound. That cap matches FindNearestSwing and the 2-swing stop branch
P6625: 6823: //--- exactly; it is a safety limit, not a tunable threshold, so Part A section 7
P6626: 6824: //--- is not engaged.
P6627: 6825: //---
P6628: 6826: //--- The opposite-direction and zone-penetration tests below are
P6629: 6827: //--- character-for-character the tests the existing single-bar S4 block already
P6630: 6828: //--- applies. Nothing new is admitted per bar. The only change is how many bars
P6631: 6829: //--- are examined.
P6632: ```
P6633:
P6634: **EX-28. Shadow retest book and shadow confirmation poll BODIES - EA 2191-2336.** New at V403, answering the V402 condition that asked for bodies rather than call sites.
P6635:
P6636: ```mql5
P6637: 2191: void ShadowRetestBook(const int barShift)
P6638: 2192:   {
P6639: 2193:    if(!InpDebugLog || !SHADOW_RETESTBOOK) return;
P6640: 2194:    double o = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6641: 2195:    double h = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6642: 2196:    double l = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6643: 2197:    double c = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6644: 2198:    if(h <= 0.0 || l <= 0.0) return;
P6645: 2199:    double cNext = (barShift >= 1) ? iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift - 1) : 0.0;
P6646: 2200:    if(cNext <= 0.0) cNext = c;
P6647: 2201:    double bodyHi = MathMax(o, cNext);
P6648: 2202:    double bodyLo = MathMin(o, cNext);
P6649: 2203:    double P   = _Point;
P6650: 2204:    double EPS = P * 0.001;
P6651: 2205:    string hits = "";
P6652: 2206:    int    nHits = 0;
P6653: 2207:    for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P6654: 2208:      {
P6655: 2209:       double L;
P6656: 2210:       if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, k, L, barShift)) continue;
P6657: 2211:       if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0)      continue;
P6658: 2212:       bool longHit  = (l <= L - P + EPS && bodyLo >= L - EPS);
P6659: 2213:       bool shortHit = (h >= L + P - EPS && bodyHi <= L + EPS);
P6660: 2214:       if(!longHit && !shortHit) continue;
P6661: 2215:       if(nHits > 0) hits += " ";
P6662: 2216:       hits += g_lineCode[k] + ":r" + IntegerToString(g_authorityRank[k]) +
P6663: 2217:               ":d" + (longHit ? "L" : "S");
P6664: 2218:       nHits++;
P6665: 2219:      }
P6666: 2220:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] RETESTBOOK bar=%s hits=%d %s",
P6667: 2221:                TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift),
P6668: 2222:                             TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6669: 2223:                nHits, hits);
P6670: 2224:     string uj_dtTerms = "";
P6671: 2225:     for(int uj_dtK = 0; uj_dtK < POI_NLINES; uj_dtK++)
P6672: 2226:       {

P6676: 2230:        string uj_dtLW = "", uj_dtSW = "";

P6678: 2232:        else if(!(bodyLo >= uj_dtL - EPS)) uj_dtLW = "body-below";
P6679: 2233:        else uj_dtLW = "HIT";
P6680: 2234:        if(!(h >= uj_dtL + P - EPS)) uj_dtSW = "no-penetration";
P6681: 2235:        else if(!(bodyHi <= uj_dtL + EPS)) uj_dtSW = "body-above";
P6682: 2236:        else uj_dtSW = "HIT";

P6684: 2238:       }

P6690: 2244: //--- SEEDFIX-1 RETESTDIAG: nearest-line census, diagnostic only. Prints beside

P6693: 2247: void ShadowRetestNearMiss(const int barShift)
P6694: 2248:    {

P6696: 2250:     double h = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6697: 2251:     double l = iLow (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6698: 2252:     if(h <= 0.0 || l <= 0.0) return;
P6699: 2253:     double P = _Point;
P6700: 2254:     string inside = "";
P6701: 2255:     string codeA = "-";
P6702: 2256:     string codeB = "-";
P6703: 2257:     double distA = 0.0;
P6704: 2258:     double distB = 0.0;
P6705: 2259:     bool haveA = false;
P6706: 2260:     bool haveB = false;
P6707: 2261:     for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P6708: 2262:       {

P6712: 2266:        if(L >= l && L <= h)
P6713: 2267:          {

P6715: 2269:           inside += g_lineCode[k];
P6716: 2270:           continue;
P6717: 2271:          }
P6718: 2272:        if(L > h && (!haveA || (L - h) < distA))
P6719: 2273:          { haveA = true; distA = L - h; codeA = g_lineCode[k]; }
P6720: 2274:        if(L < l && (!haveB || (l - L) < distB))
P6721: 2275:          { haveB = true; distB = l - L; codeB = g_lineCode[k]; }
P6722: 2276:       }
P6723: 2277:     if(StringLen(inside) == 0) inside = "-";

P6726: 2280:                              TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),

P6728: 2282:                 codeA, (haveA ? DoubleToString(distA / P, 1) : "-"),
P6729: 2283:                 codeB, (haveB ? DoubleToString(distB / P, 1) : "-"));
P6730: 2284:    }
P6731: 2285: //--- CONFIRMPOLL: the spec section 3.6 confirmation-candle terms, per bar, as data.
P6732: 2286: //--- term A oppCandle: the PRIOR candle closed AGAINST the direction (the retracement/
P6733: 2287: //---   opposing candle). term B bodyDir: the CURRENT candle closes IN the direction with
P6734: 2288: //---   any nonzero body (only a true doji rejected - the section 3.6 rule). term C
P6735: 2289: //---   touchAttr: the PRIOR candle touched the anchor line (wick through the line value).
P6736: 2290: //--- confirm = A && B && C. This is the calibration surface for build 2's
P6737: 2291: //--- IsConfirmationCandle; nothing here gates anything.
P6738: 2292: void ShadowConfirmPoll(const int barShift, const int anchorLine, const ENUM_SRJ_DIR dir)
P6739: 2293:   {
P6740: 2294:    if(!InpDebugLog || !SHADOW_CONFIRMPOLL) return;
P6741: 2295:    if(anchorLine < 0 || dir == DIR_NONE)   return;
P6742: 2296:    double o1 = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6743: 2297:    double c1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6744: 2298:    double h1 = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6745: 2299:    double l1 = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6746: 2300:    double o0 = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6747: 2301:    double c0 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6748: 2302:    if(o1 <= 0.0 || c1 <= 0.0 || o0 <= 0.0 || c0 <= 0.0) return;
P6749: 2303:    double L;
P6750: 2304:    if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, anchorLine, L, barShift)) return;
P6751: 2305:    if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0)               return;
P6752: 2306:    bool oppCandle = (dir == DIR_LONG)  ? (c1 < o1) : (c1 > o1);
P6753: 2307:    double body    = MathAbs(c0 - o0);
P6754: 2308:    bool   isDoji  = (body < _Point * 0.0001);
P6755: 2309:    bool   bodyDir = (dir == DIR_LONG)  ? (c0 > o0) : (c0 < o0);
P6756: 2310:    bool   touch   = (h1 >= L - _Point && l1 <= L + _Point);
P6757: 2311:    bool   confirm = (oppCandle && bodyDir && !isDoji && touch);
P6758: 2312:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] CONFIRMPOLL bar=%s anchor=%s dir=%s oppCandle=%d bodyDir=%d "
P6759: 2313:                "body=%dpts doji=%d touchAttr=%d confirm=%d shadow=true",
P6760: 2314:                TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift),
P6761: 2315:                             TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6762: 2316:                g_lineCode[anchorLine], DirName(dir),
P6763: 2317:                (int)oppCandle, (int)bodyDir, (int)MathRound(body / _Point),
P6764: 2318:                (int)isDoji, (int)touch, (int)confirm);
P6765: 2319:   }
P6766: 2320: 
P6767: 2321: //--- [P-CONFIRM-GATE E1 2026-09-10] build 2: the confirmation predicate is now a
P6768: 2322: //--- real function and the LIVE GATE (it replaces the poll-only role of
P6769: 2323: //--- ShadowConfirmPoll above, which keeps printing the same terms as the gate's

P6773: 2327: //---      opposing candle);
P6774: 2328: //---   A2 RULED: the prior candle's CLOSE stays on the SETUP SIDE of the anchor
P6775: 2329: //---      line (a wick through is the retracement; a CLOSE through is a line
P6776: 2330: //---      break - no confirmation after a break; LONG: close >= line;

P6778: 2332: //---   B  the current candle (barShift) closes IN dir with any nonzero body

P6782: 2336: //--- failTerm names the FIRST failed term ("" = all terms passed).
